# Graph Report - MythosMUD  (2026-08-14)

## Corpus Check
- 1232 files · ~2,966,648 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 52693 nodes · 94893 edges · 2423 communities (1797 shown, 626 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6371 edges (avg confidence: 0.57)
- Token cost: 276,209 input · 12,913 output
